Want to be notified of new patches? Hop into #hoboleaks on Tweetfleet Slack! Instructions on how to join Tweetfleet Slack here.
Please note that Singularity will be kept inaccessible except for specific testing windows, and may fall significantly behind Tranquility in patches. The Tranquility/Singularity diff (homepage) may not be particularly useful for the time being. Refer to the latest Tranquility patches instead.

96601 - Non Interactable Talocan Polestar The central piece of this Talocan station is the Polestar, the nerve center of the complex and the heart of Talocan survival. Though dilapidated and unusable, the Polestar's outer hull holds many propulsion jets and mini-generators, implying its use as a self-sufficient structure with independent capabilities. From the burn marks around the propulsion thrusters, this Polestar has been jettisoned many times as a necessary structure for a migrant culture. Non-Interactable Object

97348 - Wormhole Pilot <font size="14">Wormhole Pilot Expert System</font>

This Expert System makes available the skills a capsuleer needs to fly a gas-harvesting Venture, all Covert Ops frigates, and use combat fittings for a Praxis ready for Class 3 wormholes.

The temporary skills provided by this Expert System last for seven days.

An Expert System is a remote, restricted artificial intelligence providing you with access to certain skills during the active rental period. As it is independent from your clone and your capsule, an active Expert System is not lost on pod death or clone jump.

<font size="14">Activating an Expert System</font>

Activation of an Expert System is automatic upon redemption of the item by the specific capsuleer intending to use the service.

Expert Systems are a product developed by the PKN Interstellar consortium, made available over the HyperNet FTL communications network, and marketed by PKN Interstellar member corporations. An Expert System is a restricted AI based on artificial gelbrain technology. As the Expert System uses neural pathways based on those created by skill training or skill injection, it is compatible with capsule-ship interface and control systems. Real time command and control communication is achieved using dedicated FTL channels on PKN Interstellar's HyperNet.		 Standard Expert Systems

679073 Angel Mining Op Ambush ADV-10 Angel Mining Op Ambush ADV-5 Angel Mining Op Ambush ADV-105
679541 During Pirate Insurgencies, pirate factions face off against the empires, each side attempting to 'score' systems by increasing the system's Corruption (pirates) or Suppression (militias) value through the completion of various activities and objectives. Each faction has an independent target score they are looking to reach to allow them to win the insurgency, with the minimum score required to win increasing for a faction as they win multiple events.

An insurgency starts with the placement of a pirate Forward Operating Base (FOB) in the warzone. Several systems are then selected adjacent to the FOB system as initial insurgency targets. This begins the ‘Forecasting Period'. The insurgency does not become immediately active, giving both pirate and militia capsuleers time to move ships into position and otherwise prepare themselves for the event.

Once the forecasting period ends, the main phase of the Insurgency begins. Insurgency activities begin spawning and both pirate and militia players are able to contest them. Each Insurgency activity is worth a number off Corruption or Suppression points. As pirates complete objectives they will increase the Corruption value of the system; as militia players complete insurgency activities they will increase the Suppression value of the system. A system reaching maximum Corruption scores a point for the pirates while a system reaching maximum Suppression scores a point for the empires.

When the Corruption value of a system hits stage 4 during an Insurgency event, the insurgency will spread - adding a new system to the growing insurgency zone - a new target for the pirates. Activities will begin spawning in this system immediately. This dynamic spread will prefer systems adjacent to the system which triggered the spread if possible and will not spread to systems with a security status of 0.8 and higher or 0.0 and lower.

When the pirates reach their target score they are deemed the winners of the insurgency. All systems with active insurgency will see their Corruption value increased to the next stage and the pirates will seize the FOB system as their own - removing it from the FW warzone and causing neighboring systems to become frontlines (see Factional Warfare guide for further information on frontline status). After some time, the pirates will then abandon this system, tenuously returning it to the previous owner with control at minimum and the infrastructure hub vulnerable.

If the empires reach their target score however, then the pirate FOB becomes vulnerable. The FOB has a single armor reinforcement timer and, when destroyed, will result in defeat for the pirates and victory for the empires. Following a victory by the empires, all systems in the insurgency will see their Suppression value raise by one level for some time before the event comes to a close.

There are additional rewards for players on both sides who participate in an insurgency. 697080 Pilots will receive LP from their enlisted faction for successful completion of an Mining Op Ambush. LP rewards will be evenly divided among all participants aligned to the winning faction who are present upon completion. Some of these sites may also include reward scaling, with an increasing total reward up to a certain number of pilots – this is indicated by the number included in the name of the site such as 'ADV-10', indicating the reward scales up to 10 pilots before individual payouts begin to decrease.

Completion of these activities will also contribute to the pilot’s individual contribution rewards pool. As the pilot generates corruption or suppression for their faction during insurgencies, additional LP and ISK rewards will be paid out upon completion of the insurgency, with larger rewards available to pilots aligned with the winning faction. 1053046 Capacitor Cost and Zones Capacitor Cost and Zones UI/Inflight/SpaceComponents/JumpBridge/Info/CapacitorUsage
1053047 When a jump is initiated at an Ansiblex, it consumes some of that Ansiblex’s capacitor charge.
The amount consumed is equal to the ship's base Ansiblex Activation Cost<b/> multiplied by the Zone multiplier of the destination system.
The destination system’s Zone is defined by its distance from the alliance’s Capital system.
Zone 1: 0 to 5 ly
Zone 2: 5.1 to 10 ly
Zone 3: 10.1 to 15 ly
Zone 4: 15.1 to 20 ly
Zone 5: 20.1 ly and above
The multipliers for each zone can be found in the attributes tab.
A newly anchored Ansiblex’s capacitor charge starts at 0 and recharges over time.		 When a jump is initiated at an Ansiblex, it consumes some of that Ansiblex’s capacitor charge.

1053977 Wormhole Pilot Wormhole Pilot Name of item 97348 (Standard Expert Systems)
1053978 <font size="14">Wormhole Pilot Expert System</font>

This Expert System makes available the skills a capsuleer needs to fly a gas-harvesting Venture, all Covert Ops frigates, and use combat fittings for a Praxis ready for Class 3 wormholes.

The temporary skills provided by this Expert System last for seven days.

An Expert System is a remote, restricted artificial intelligence providing you with access to certain skills during the active rental period. As it is independent from your clone and your capsule, an active Expert System is not lost on pod death or clone jump.

<font size="14">Activating an Expert System</font>

Activation of an Expert System is automatic upon redemption of the item by the specific capsuleer intending to use the service.

Expert Systems are a product developed by the PKN Interstellar consortium, made available over the HyperNet FTL communications network, and marketed by PKN Interstellar member corporations. An Expert System is a restricted AI based on artificial gelbrain technology. As the Expert System uses neural pathways based on those created by skill training or skill injection, it is compatible with capsule-ship interface and control systems. Real time command and control communication is achieved using dedicated FTL channels on PKN Interstellar's HyperNet.		 <font size="14"><b>Wormhole Pilot Expert System</b></font>

1054411 Insurgent Insurgent Name of item 97406 (Standard Expert Systems)
1054412 <font size="14">Insurgent Expert System</font>

This Expert System makes available the skills a capsuleer needs to fly an effective battlecruiser in Insurgency complexes.

The temporary skills provided by this Expert System last for seven days.

An Expert System is a remote, restricted artificial intelligence providing you with access to certain skills during the active rental period. As it is independent from your clone and your capsule, an active Expert System is not lost on pod death or clone jump.

<font size="14">Activating an Expert System</font>

Activation of an Expert System is automatic upon redemption of the item by the specific capsuleer intending to use the service.

Expert Systems are a product developed by the PKN Interstellar consortium, made available over the HyperNet FTL communications network, and marketed by PKN Interstellar member corporations. An Expert System is a restricted AI based on artificial gelbrain technology. As the Expert System uses neural pathways based on those created by skill training or skill injection, it is compatible with capsule-ship interface and control systems. Real-time command and control communication is achieved using dedicated FTL channels on PKN Interstellar's HyperNet.		 <font size="14"><b>Insurgent Expert System</b></font>

1055410 This beacon marks a moderate Corporate Outpost location. The entry gate is configured to admit Tech I, Tech II and all faction ships of Battlecruiser size or smaller.

The security features installed in this acceleration gate will prevent activation by any ship that is fitted with warp core stabilizer modules.
